1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the dna space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 113 and the convergence rate is O(247/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the dna space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 113 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 247. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any dna module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 247. The projective resolution has length at most 302 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 247 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dna structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 302. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
